# Blended values: preview

> **[Beta]** Blended values are built by automated steps without manual human verification. Each value names the tables, codes and published cells it came from. Not yet used by the planner.

Generated by `blend.py`; don't edit.

142 foods are the same food (or equivalent) in USDA SR Legacy and CoFID 2021 (`../matches/cofid.csv`). For each definition the value is the **median** of the tables that publish it under that definition; with two tables the median is their average. Values under different definitions are never combined, so some nutrients come from one table only.

- Values more than 2-fold apart (flagged, not dropped): 521
- Foods whose CoFID record is borrowed from USDA data (counted once): 13 (Almonds, Lemon peel, raw, Lime juice, raw, Mushrooms, shiitake, dried, Mushrooms, shiitake, cooked, without salt, Taro leaves, cooked, steamed, without salt, Arrowhead, raw, Arrowhead, cooked, boiled, drained, without salt, Chard, swiss, raw, Chives, raw, Chard, swiss, cooked, boiled, drained, without salt, Drumstick pods, raw, Drumstick pods, cooked, boiled, drained, without salt)

Definitions used below:

- **Energy (US Atwater factors)**: Energy from protein, fat and carbohydrate (by difference) with Atwater food-specific factors; for multi-ingredient processed foods, industry factors 4/4/9 (kcal)
- **Energy (UK factors)**: Energy from protein 4, fat 9, available carbohydrate as monosaccharide equivalents 3.75 and alcohol 7 kcal/g; no energy from fibre (the UK convention; not the EU labelling factors) (kcal)
- **Energy (EU labelling factors)**: Energy with the EU labelling factors (Regulation 1169/2011, Annex XIV): protein 4, fat 9, carbohydrate by weight 4, fibre 2 kcal/g; calculated from blended parts. Polyols and organic acids are counted as carbohydrate (4, not 2.4 or 3); foods with alcohol aren't calculated (kcal)
- **Protein**: Total nitrogen x a food-specific conversion factor (6.25 unless stated); non-protein nitrogen removed for mushrooms (g)
- **Fat**: Total fat (all lipid components extracted), not only triglycerides (g)
- **Carbohydrate (by difference, without fibre)**: Available carbohydrate by difference: 100 minus water, protein, fat, ash, alcohol and total dietary fibre (g)
- **Carbohydrate (available, as monosaccharides)**: Available carbohydrate as the sum of analysed starch, sugars and oligosaccharides, in monosaccharide equivalents (about 5-10% above weight for starch and disaccharides) (g)
- **Fibre (AOAC)**: Total dietary fibre by AOAC enzymatic-gravimetric methods (includes resistant starch and lignin) (g)
- **Sugars (by weight)**: Total free mono- and disaccharides, by weight (g)
- **Sugars (as monosaccharides)**: Total free mono- and disaccharides in monosaccharide equivalents (disaccharides counted about 5% heavier) (g)
- **Sodium**: Sodium (mg)
- **Calcium**: Calcium (mg)
- **Iron**: Iron (mg)
- **Vitamin A (retinol activity equivalents)**: Retinol activity equivalents: retinol + beta-carotene/12 + other provitamin A carotenoids/24 (IOM 2001) (µg)
- **Vitamin A (retinol equivalents)**: Retinol equivalents: retinol + beta-carotene/6 + other provitamin A carotenoids/12 (µg)
- **Folate (total)**: Total folate (food folate + any folic acid), by weight (µg)
- **Thiamin**: Thiamin. CoFID expresses it as thiamine chloride hydrochloride; USDA doesn't state the form (combined on a data check: caveat salt-basis-thiamin) (mg)
- **Vitamin B6**: Vitamin B6. CoFID expresses it as pyridoxine hydrochloride; USDA doesn't state the form (combined on a data check: caveat salt-basis-vitaminB6) (mg)
- **Pantothenic acid**: Pantothenic acid. CoFID expresses it as calcium D-pantothenate; USDA doesn't state the form (combined on a data check: caveat salt-basis-pantothenic) (mg)

## Caveats

Shown with every value they apply to (the same labels as on the site).

> **[Assumption]** `salt-basis-thiamin`. Combined on a data check. CoFID says it expresses thiamin as thiamine chloride hydrochloride; USDA doesn't say. If the two differed, CoFID's values would be about 27% higher (molar masses 337.3 and 265.4). Applies to: Thiamin. Reason: The documents don't settle the basis; owner's decision (18 Nov 2026) to combine on the data check, with this caveat shown. Evidence: Across 121 foods matched in both tables, CoFID's value was a median 1.08 times USDA's (middle half 0.76–1.78): no sign of a 1.27 factor (checked 2026-11-18).

> **[Assumption]** `salt-basis-vitaminB6`. Combined on a data check. CoFID says it expresses vitamin B6 as pyridoxine hydrochloride; USDA doesn't say. If the two differed, CoFID's values would be about 22% higher (molar masses 205.6 and 169.2). Applies to: Vitamin B6. Reason: The documents don't settle the basis; owner's decision (18 Nov 2026) to combine on the data check, with this caveat shown. Evidence: Across 114 foods matched in both tables, CoFID's value was a median 0.96 times USDA's (middle half 0.54–1.23): no sign of a 1.22 factor (checked 2026-11-18).

> **[Assumption]** `salt-basis-pantothenic`. Combined on a data check. CoFID says it expresses pantothenate as calcium D-pantothenate; USDA reports pantothenic acid and doesn't say. If the two differed, CoFID's values would be about 9% higher (238.3 and 219.2 per pantothenate). Applies to: Pantothenic acid. Reason: The documents don't settle the basis; owner's decision (18 Nov 2026) to combine on the data check, with this caveat shown. Evidence: Across 112 foods matched in both tables, CoFID's value was a median 1.00 times USDA's (middle half 0.74–1.50): no sign of a 1.09 factor (checked 2026-11-18).

> **[Borrowed]** `borrowed-record`. One of these records names another table we use as its main data source, so its values count once: they are shown, but not used in the median. Reason: Avoids counting one measurement twice (borrowed.csv).

> **[Assumption]** `other-definition`. This value uses a different definition from the one this standard's target uses, because the tables don't give that one for this food. Reason: Better than leaving it missing; the definition used is named with the value.

> **[Not yet checked]** `definition-not-checked`. Which definition this standard's target uses isn't stated in the archived documents; the choice follows the standard's usual practice. Reason: The defining report isn't archived yet (see standards.csv).

> **[Assumption]** `closest-definition`. The US target is for total fibre (dietary plus functional fibre); food tables measure fibre by AOAC methods, which capture most of it but not all. Reason: The closest definition food tables give. Evidence: NASEM DRI macronutrients summary (Total Fiber); SACN 2015, paragraphs 2.32–2.33 (checked 2026-11-18).

## By standard

Which definition each standard's targets use (`../standards.csv`). The planner takes the first one a food has; a later one carries the *other-definition* caveat, and an *assumed* choice the *definition-not-checked* caveat. Nutrients not listed have one definition.

| Standard | Nutrient | Definitions, in order | How established | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UK | energy | Energy (UK factors); Energy (US Atwater factors) | derived | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf, Table 2: carbohydrate 333 g = 50% of 2,500 kcal / 3.75 kcal per g, the UK factor (cofid2021_McCance_and_Widdowsons_Composition_of_Foods_integrated_dataset_2021_user_guide.pdf, Energy value) |
| UK | carbohydrate | Carbohydrate (available, as monosaccharides); Carbohydrate (by difference, without fibre); Carbohydrate (by difference, with fibre) | derived | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf, Table 2: 333 g at 3.75 kcal/g means available carbohydrate as monosaccharide equivalents |
| UK | sugars | Sugars (as monosaccharides); Sugars (by weight) | no target | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf, Tables 1 and 2: the target is free sugars, which food tables don't separate |
| UK | vitaminA | Vitamin A (retinol equivalents); Vitamin A (retinol activity equivalents) | confirmed | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf, vitamin figures from Dietary Reference Values (1991); cofid2021_McCance_and_Widdowsons_Composition_of_Foods_integrated_dataset_2021_user_guide.pdf, Total retinol equivalent, cites that report for retinol equivalents |
| UK | vitaminE | Vitamin E (alpha-tocopherol equivalents); Vitamin E (alpha-tocopherol) | no target | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf: no vitamin E recommendation |
| UK | vitaminD | Vitamin D (D2 + D3) | confirmed | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf, vitamin D from SACN Vitamin D and Health (2016), in µg |
| UK | folate | Folate (total); Folate (dietary folate equivalents) | confirmed | phe-2016-government-dietary-recommendations.pdf, Folate (µg/day); from Dietary Reference Values (1991), before dietary folate equivalents existed |
| UK | fibre | Fibre (AOAC) | confirmed | sacn2015_SACN_Carbohydrates_and_Health.pdf, recommendations S.21–S.22: dietary fibre 'chemically determined using the prevailing AOAC methods'; the 30 g/day value 'measured using the AOAC methods' |
| US | energy | Energy (US Atwater factors); Energy (UK factors) | convention | Energy targets are needs, not a food-table definition; US intakes are measured with USDA's own factors (usda-sr28-documentation.pdf, Food Energy) |
| US | carbohydrate | Carbohydrate (by difference, without fibre); Carbohydrate (available, as monosaccharides); Carbohydrate (by difference, with fibre) | confirmed | nasem-dri-macronutrients-summary.pdf, Carbohydrate row: total digestible carbohydrate; starch and sugar are the major types |
| US | sugars | Sugars (by weight); Sugars (as monosaccharides) | no target | Dietary Guidelines limit added sugars, which food tables don't separate |
| US | vitaminA | Vitamin A (retinol activity equivalents); Vitamin A (retinol equivalents) | confirmed | nasem-dri-rda-ai-vitamins-elements.pdf, footnote: as retinol activity equivalents (RAEs) |
| US | vitaminE | Vitamin E (alpha-tocopherol); Vitamin E (alpha-tocopherol equivalents) | confirmed | nasem-dri-rda-ai-vitamins-elements.pdf, footnote: as alpha-tocopherol |
| US | vitaminD | Vitamin D (D2 + D3) | confirmed | nasem-dri-rda-ai-vitamins-elements.pdf, footnote: as cholecalciferol, µg |
| US | folate | Folate (dietary folate equivalents); Folate (total) | confirmed | nasem-dri-rda-ai-vitamins-elements.pdf, footnote: as dietary folate equivalents (DFE) |
| US | fibre | Fibre (AOAC) | closest | nasem-dri-macronutrients-summary.pdf, Total Fiber row: includes dietary fiber and functional fiber; sacn2015_SACN_Carbohydrates_and_Health.pdf, paragraphs 2.32–2.33: AOAC methods 985.29 and 991.43 measure non-starch polysaccharides, some resistant starch, lignin and some inulin |
| EU | energy | Energy (EU labelling factors); Energy (UK factors); Energy (US Atwater factors) | confirmed | eu-2011-regulation-1169.pdf, Annex XIV: carbohydrate (except polyols) 4, protein 4, fat 9, alcohol 7, fibre 2 kcal/g |
| EU | carbohydrate | Carbohydrate (by difference, without fibre); Carbohydrate (available, as monosaccharides); Carbohydrate (by difference, with fibre) | confirmed | eu-2011-regulation-1169.pdf, Annex I point 7: 'any carbohydrate which is metabolised by humans, and includes polyols' (by weight); efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 3.2 |
| EU | sugars | Sugars (by weight); Sugars (as monosaccharides) | no target | efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 3.2: no reference value for sugars |
| EU | vitaminA | Vitamin A (retinol equivalents); Vitamin A (retinol activity equivalents) | confirmed | efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 5.9: in µg retinol equivalents (1 µg RE = 1 µg retinol, 6 µg beta-carotene, 12 µg other carotenoids) |
| EU | vitaminE | Vitamin E (alpha-tocopherol); Vitamin E (alpha-tocopherol equivalents) | confirmed | efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 5.13: vitamin E as alpha-tocopherol only |
| EU | vitaminD | Vitamin D (D2 + D3) | confirmed | efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 5.12: in µg |
| EU | folate | Folate (dietary folate equivalents); Folate (total) | confirmed | efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 5.4: in µg dietary folate equivalents (DFE) |
| EU | fibre | Fibre (AOAC) | confirmed | efsa-2017-drv-summary-report.pdf, section 3.2: non-digestible carbohydrates plus lignin, including resistant starch and oligosaccharides |

## Foods

The full trail for every value (table, file checksum, code, column, published cell) is in `audit-cofid.csv`; all definitions are in `blended-cofid.json`.
